Through a rigorous evaluation of 18 LLMs, we reveal a significant proficiency gap: even state-of-the-art LLMs struggle with complex historical research questions. We hope ProHist-Bench will facilitate the development of domain-specific reasoning LLMs, advance computational historical research, and further uncover the untapped potential of LLMs. We release ProHist-Bench at https://github.com/inclusionAI/ABench/tree/main/ProHist-Bench.

</details>


### [252] [Contextual Linear Activation Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24693)
*Brandon Hsu,Daniel Beaglehole,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLAS是一种动态调整线性激活引导强度的上下文自适应方法，在有限标注数据下持续优于标准线性激活引导，性能可与ReFT和LoRA媲美或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性激活引导方法对所有token使用固定引导强度，导致不同输入提示下的引导质量不一致，需要一种上下文自适应的引导方法。

Method: 提出上下文线性激活引导（CLAS），动态调整线性激活引导的强度以适应不同的上下文。

Result: 在11个引导基准测试和4个模型家族上，CLAS持续优于标准线性激活引导，在有限标注数据设置下匹配或超过ReFT和LoRA的性能。

Conclusion: CLAS是一种可扩展、可解释且精确的大语言模型专化与引导方法。

Abstract: Linear activation steering is a powerful approach for eliciting the capabilities of large language models and specializing their behavior using limited labeled data. While effective, existing methods often apply a fixed steering strength to all tokens, resulting in inconsistent steering quality across diverse input prompts. In this work, we introduce Contextual Linear Activation Steering (CLAS), a method that dynamically adapts linear activation steering to context-dependent steering strengths. Across eleven steering benchmarks and four model families, it consistently outperforms standard linear activation steering and matches or exceeds the performance of ReFT and LoRA in settings with limited labeled data. We therefore propose CLAS as a scalable, interpretable, and accurate method for specializing and steering large language models.

</details>


### [253] [Green Shielding: A User-Centric Approach Towards Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24700)
*Aaron J. Li,Nicolas Sanchez,Hao Huang,Ruijiang Dong,Jaskaran Bains,Katrin Jaradeh,Zhen Xiang,Bo Li,Feng Liu,Aaron Kornblith,Bin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Green Shielding议程，通过CUE标准构建基于证据的部署指南，研究良性输入变化如何影响模型行为。在医疗诊断领域通过HealthCareMagic-Diagnosis基准实例化，发现提示层面因素会沿临床意义维度改变模型行为，并呈现帕累托式权衡，例如neutralization操作增加合理性和简洁性但降低了对高风险病症的覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试未能充分解决非对抗性、日常性的用户查询措辞变化对LLM输出的影响，缺乏系统性的用户侧部署指导。

Method: 提出Green Shielding议程，通过CUE标准（真实语境的基准、真实效用的参考标准和指标、反映真实输入变化的扰动）操作化实现。基于PCS框架，与执业医师合作在医疗诊断领域构建HCM-Dx基准，包含患者撰写的查询、结构化参考诊断集和临床驱动的指标，并研究提示层面扰动。

Result: 多个前沿LLM上，输入变化沿临床意义维度改变模型行为并呈现帕累托式权衡。neutralization操作增加了输出合理性和简洁性，但降低了对高风险和高度可能病症的覆盖。

Conclusion: 交互选择可以系统性地改变模型输出的任务相关属性，支持在高风险领域构建面向用户的部署指南。该议程可扩展至其他决策支持和自主AI系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, yet their outputs can be highly sensitive to routine, non-adversarial variation in how users phrase queries, a gap not well addressed by existing red-teaming efforts. We propose Green Shielding, a user-centric agenda for building evidence-backed deployment guidance by characterizing how benign input variation shifts model behavior. We operationalize this agenda through the CUE criteria: benchmarks with authentic Context, reference standards and metrics that capture true Utility, and perturbations that reflect realistic variations in the Elicitation of model behavior. Guided by the PCS framework and developed with practicing physicians, we instantiate Green Shielding in medical diagnosis through HealthCareMagic-Diagnosis (HCM-Dx), a benchmark of patient-authored queries, together with structured reference diagnosis sets and clinically grounded metrics for evaluating differential diagnosis lists. We also study perturbation regimes that capture routine input variation and show that prompt-level factors shift model behavior along clinically meaningful dimensions. Across multiple frontier LLMs, these shifts trace out Pareto-like tradeoffs. In particular, neutralization, which removes common user-level factors while preserving clinical content, increases plausibility and yields more concise, clinician-like differentials, but reduces coverage of highly likely and safety-critical conditions. Together, these results show that interaction choices can systematically shift task-relevant properties of model outputs and support user-facing guidance for safer deployment in high-stakes domains. Although instantiated here in medical diagnosis, the agenda extends naturally to other decision-support settings and agentic AI systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [PExA: Parallel Exploration Agent for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22934)
*Tanmay Parekh,Ella Hofmann-Coyle,Shuyi Wang,Sachith Sri Ram Kothur,Srivas Prasad,Yunmo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于软件测试覆盖思想的方法，将原始SQL查询分解为多个原子SQL测试用例并行执行，在充分覆盖语义后生成最终SQL，在Spider 2.0上达到70.2%的执行准确率，创下新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based文本到SQL智能体在延迟与性能之间存在权衡，性能提升往往以延迟增加为代价，反之亦然。现有方法难以同时优化这两个指标。

Method: 将文本到SQL生成重新形式化为软件测试覆盖问题：为原始查询准备一组包含更简单、原子化SQL的测试用例，并行执行这些用例以确保对原始查询的语义覆盖；通过迭代测试用例覆盖，仅在收集足够信息后生成最终SQL，利用已探索的测试用例SQL来支撑最终生成。

Result: 在Spider 2.0基准上达到70.2%的执行准确率，取得了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂查询分解为并行执行的原子测试用例，该方法有效缓解了延迟-性能权衡问题，在保证高性能的同时实现了高效的查询生成。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for text-to-SQL often struggle with latency-performance trade-off, where performance improvements come at the cost of latency or vice versa. We reformulate text-to-SQL generation within the lens of software test coverage where the original query is prepared with a suite of test cases with simpler, atomic SQLs that are executed in parallel and together ensure semantic coverage of the original query. After iterating on test case coverage, the final SQL is generated only when enough information is gathered, leveraging the explored test case SQLs to ground the final generation. We validated our framework on a state-of-the-art benchmark for text-to-SQL, Spider 2.0, achieving a new state-of-the-art with 70.2% execution accuracy.

</details>


### [255] [The Power of Power Law: Asymmetry Enables Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22951)
*Zixuan Wang,Xingyu Dang,Jason D. Lee,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个反直觉的结果：在组合推理任务中，幂律分布训练持续优于均匀分布训练。理论分析表明幂律采样引入了一种有益的不对称性，改善了病态的损失景观，使模型先学习高频组合技能作为基石，从而更高效地学习罕见长尾技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据遵循幂律分布，大多数知识和技能出现频率极低。传统直觉认为对数据进行重加权或筛选使其趋于均匀分布有助于模型学习长尾技能，但这一直觉缺乏深入验证。

Method: 在多种组合推理任务（状态追踪、多步算术）上进行实验比较幂律分布与均匀分布训练效果；并设计了一个最小化技能组合任务进行理论分析，证明幂律分布下学习所需数据量显著更少。

Result: 幂律分布训练在组合推理任务中一致优于均匀分布训练；理论分析证明幂律采样所需训练数据显著更少。

Conclusion: 幂律采样引入有益的不对称性，改善了损失景观，使模型先学高频技能组合作为基石，再高效学习罕见长尾技能，为有效数据分布设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Natural language data follows a power-law distribution, with most knowledge and skills appearing at very low frequency. While a common intuition suggests that reweighting or curating data towards a uniform distribution may help models better learn these long-tail skills, we find a counterintuitive result: across a wide range of compositional reasoning tasks, such as state tracking and multi-step arithmetic, training under power-law distributions consistently outperforms training under uniform distributions. To understand this advantage, we introduce a minimalist skill-composition task and show that learning under a power-law distribution provably requires significantly less training data. Our theoretical analysis reveals that power law sampling induces a beneficial asymmetry that improves the pathological loss landscape, which enables models to first acquire high-frequency skill compositions with low data complexity, which in turn serves as a stepping stone to efficiently learn rare long-tailed skills. Our results offer an alternative perspective on what constitutes an effective data distribution for training models.

</details>


### [256] [On the Existence of an Inverse Solution for Preference-Based Reductions in Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22958)
*Alessio Zaninotto,Bruno Yun,Nir Oren,Srdjan Vesic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究偏好论证框架（PAFs）的逆问题：给定论证图、标记和语义，判断是否存在偏好关系能产生所需标记。在四种常见偏好约简下，该问题大多可在多项式时间内解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PAFs通过偏好控制攻击向击败的转换，但现有研究多关注正向推理（给定偏好求结果），而反向问题（给定期望结果求偏好）在偏好 elicitation 和可解释性中有重要应用，尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在完全语义下，针对四种最常用的基于偏好的约简方法，将逆问题形式化为判定是否存在偏好关系使得给定标记成立，并分析每种情况的计算复杂度。

Result: 在大多数情况下，该逆问题可以在多项式时间内判定，表明其计算可行性良好。

Conclusion: 偏好论证框架的逆问题在多数约简下是多项式时间可解的，这为偏好 elicitation 和可解释性等应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Preference-based argumentation frameworks (PAFs) extend Dung's approach to abstract argumentation (AAFs) by encoding preferences over arguments. Such preferences control the transformation of attacks into defeats, and different approaches to doing so result in different reductions from a PAF to an AAF. In this paper we consider a PAF inverse problem which takes an argumentation graph, a labelling and a semantics as an input, and outputs a ``yes" or ``no" as to whether there is a preference relation between the arguments which can yield the desired labelling. This inverse problem has applications in areas including preference elicitation and explainability. We consider this problem in the context of the four most widely-used preference based reductions under the complete semantics. We show that in most cases, the problem can be answered in polynomial time.

</details>


### [257] [FormalScience: Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Autoformalisation of Science with Agentic Code Generation in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23002)
*Jordan Meadows,Lan Zhang,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FormalScience——一个人机协同的自动化形式化流水线，使领域专家无需深厚形式语言经验即可将非正式的科学推理（以物理为例）转换为语法正确、语义对齐的Lean4形式证明。基于此构建了包含200道大学物理题（量子力学与电磁学）的FormalPhysics数据集，在形式有效性和语句复杂度上超越现有形式数学基准。研究还系统刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移现象（如符号坍缩、抽象提升），并评估了多种LLM方法在语句自动形式化任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非正式的数学推理形式化为可验证代码对大型语言模型而言极具挑战，而科学领域（如物理）中的专用符号体系（如狄拉克符号、矢量微积分）带来了额外困难。当前LLM及智能体方法尚未有效解决这些挑战，因此需要一种降低形式化门槛、支持科学领域自动形式化的方法。

Method: 提出FormalScience——一种领域无关的人机协同智能体流水线，使单一领域专家（无需深厚形式语言经验）能以低成本生成语法正确且语义对齐的形式证明。将该方法应用于物理领域，构建FormalPhysics数据集（200道大学级LaTeX物理问题及其Lean4形式表示）。在语句自动形式化任务中评估了零样本提示、带错误反馈的自精炼以及多阶段智能体方法。此外，首次系统刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移现象，包括符号坍缩和抽象提升等概念。

Result: FormalPhysics数据集相比现有形式数学基准实现了完美的形式有效性，并展现出更高的语句复杂度。通过对开源模型和商业系统在自动形式化任务上的评估，揭示了当前LLM方法在自动形式化方面的局限性。首次提供了对物理自动形式化中语义漂移的系统性刻画。

Conclusion: FormalScience能够有效帮助领域专家将非正式科学推理形式化为可验证代码。FormalPhysics数据集为物理领域的形式化研究提供了高质量基准。对语义漂移的系统刻画揭示了形式语言在无法完全保留语义时实际验证的内容。该方法可推广至物理之外的更多科学领域。

Abstract: Formalising informal mathematical reasoning into formally verifiable code is a significant challenge for large language models. In scientific fields such as physics, domain-specific machinery (\textit{e.g.} Dirac notation, vector calculus) imposes additional formalisation challenges that modern LLMs and agentic approaches have yet to tackle. To aid autoformalisation in scientific domains, we present FormalScience; a domain-agnostic human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline that enables a single domain expert (without deep formal language experience) to produce \textit{syntactically correct} and \textit{semantically aligned} formal proofs of informal reasoning for low economic cost. Applying FormalScience to physics, we construct FormalPhysics, a dataset of 200 university-level (LaTeX) physics problems and solutions (primarily quantum mechanics and electromagnetism), along with their Lean4 formal representations. Compared to existing formal math benchmarks, FormalPhysics achieves perfect formal validity and exhibits greater statement complexity. We evaluate open-source models and proprietary systems on a statement autoformalisation task on our dataset via zero-shot prompting, self-refinement with error feedback, and a novel multi-stage agentic approach, and explore autoformalisation limitations in modern LLM-based approaches. We provide the first systematic characterisation of semantic drift in physics autoformalisation in terms of concepts such as notational collapse and abstraction elevation which reveals what formal language verifies when full semantic preservation is unattainable. We release the codebase together with an interactive UI-based FormalScience system which facilitates autoformalisation and theorem proving in scientific domains beyond physics.https://github.com/jmeadows17/formal-science

</details>


### [258] [A Systematic Approach for Large Language Models Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23027)
*Basel Shbita,Anna Lisa Gentile,Bing Zhang,Sungeun An,Shailja Thakur,Shubhi Asthana,Yi Zhou,Saptha Surendran,Farhan Ahmed,Rohan Kulkarni,Yuya Jeremy Ong,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的LLM调试方法，将模型视为可观测系统，通过统一的评估、可解释性和错误分析实践，实现从问题检测到模型优化的迭代式诊断与改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM固有的黑箱性、概率性和跨任务诊断困难导致调试过程缺乏系统性和可复现性，现有方法难以有效应对多样化场景下的错误分析与模型优化需求。

Method: 提出一种模型无关的、结构化的调试框架，统一评估、可解释性和错误分析实践，从问题检测到模型优化形成迭代闭环，支持提示词调整、参数优化和微调数据适配。

Result: 该方法能加速故障排查，提升LLM系统的可复现性、透明度和可扩展性，尤其在缺乏标准化基准和评估标准的情境下依然有效。

Conclusion: 系统化的调试方法论对LLM的可靠部署至关重要，能够有效弥合模型黑箱性与实际应用需求之间的鸿沟，推动LLM系统的可观测性和可维护性建设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become central to modern AI workflows, powering applications from open-ended text generation to complex agent-based reasoning. However, debugging these models remains a persistent challenge due to their opaque and probabilistic nature and the difficulty of diagnosing errors across diverse tasks and settings. This paper introduces a systematic approach for LLM debugging that treats models as observable systems, providing structured, model-agnostic methods from issue detection to model refinement. By unifying evaluation, interpretability, and error-analysis practices, our approach enables practitioners to iteratively diagnose model weaknesses, refine prompts and model parameters, and adapt data for fine-tuning or assessment, while remaining effective in contexts where standardized benchmarks and evaluation criteria are lacking. We argue that such a structured methodology not only accelerates troubleshooting but also fosters reproducibility, transparency, and scalability in the deployment of LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [259] [Don't Make the LLM Read the Graph: Make the Graph Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23057)
*Yuqi Sun,Tianqin Meng,George Liu,Yashraj Panwar,Lakshya Chaudhry,Munasib Ilham,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过3000+次受控实验，研究了显式信念图是否提升LLM在合作多智能体推理中的表现。主要发现包括：集成架构决定信念图价值（提示上下文仅对弱模型有益，而门控排序对强模型也至关重要）；识别出"规划者反抗"这一模型家族特异性失败模式（Llama 70B拒绝率90%，Gemini接近零）；完整游戏证据表明智能体间约定优于单智能体干预；初步缩放分析显示图深度收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式信念图（explicit belief graphs）能否提升大型语言模型在合作多智能体推理任务中的表现，尤其是在需要高阶心智理论（Theory of Mind）的场景下。

Method: 在合作纸牌游戏Hanabi中，对四个LLM家族进行3000+次受控实验。设计了两种集成架构（提示上下文 vs. 门控排序），测试不同阶次心智理论任务，并进行完整游戏评估和初步缩放分析。

Result: 1) 集成架构决定信念图效果：作为提示上下文仅对弱模型有益（2阶ToM准确率80% vs 10%，OR=36.0），门控排序对强模型也至关重要（100% vs 20%）。2) 发现"规划者反抗"：Llama 70B在部分胜任时90%覆写正确规划建议，Gemini近乎零。3) 完整游戏中智能体间约定比基线提升+128%。4) 图深度收益递减，浅层图性价比最高，深层图在5人局中表现更差（-1.5pts）。

Conclusion: 显式信念图的价值高度依赖于集成方式；智能体间约定优于单智能体优化；模型家族存在特异性失败模式（规划者反抗）；信念图深度并非越深越好，存在收益递减效应。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit belief graphs improve LLM performance in cooperative multi-agent reasoning. Through 3,000+ controlled trials across four LLM families in the cooperative card game Hanabi, we establish four findings. First, integration architecture determines whether belief graphs provide value: as prompt context, graphs are decorative for strong models and beneficial only for weak models on 2nd-order Theory of Mind (80% vs 10%, p<0.0001, OR=36.0); when graphs gate action selection through ranked shortlists, they become structurally essential even for strong models (100% vs 20% on 2nd-order ToM, p<0.001). Second, we identify "Planner Defiance," a model-family-specific failure where LLMs override correct planner recommendations at partial competence (90% override, replicated N=20); Gemini models show near-zero defiance while Llama 70B shows 90%, and models distinguish factual context (deferred to) from advisory recommendations (overridden). Third, full-game evidence confirms inter-agent conventions (+128% over baseline, p=0.003) outperform all single-agent interventions, and individual belief-graph components must be combined to produce gains. Fourth, preliminary scaling analysis (N=10/cell, exploratory) suggests graph depth has diminishing returns: shallow graphs provide the best cost-benefit ratio, while deeper ToM graphs appear harmful at larger player counts (-1.5 pts at 5-player, p=0.029).

</details>


### [260] [Analytica: Soft Propositional Reasoning for Robust and Scalable LLM-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23072)
*Junyan Cheng,Kyle Richardson,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytica，一种基于软命题推理（SPR）的智能体架构，通过将复杂分析转化为结构化子命题的软真值估计过程，结合并行分治框架、工具增强型LLM接地智能体和稳健线性模型，显著降低了推理的偏差和方差。在金融、经济和政治预测任务上实现了15.84%的平均准确率提升（最高71.06%），同时方差最低（6.02%），并展现出良好的成本效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂真实世界分析（如金融预测、科学发现）中存在随机性不稳定、缺乏可验证的组合结构等问题，缺乏一种能系统性减少推理偏差和方差的形式化框架。

Method: 提出软命题推理（SPR）形式化框架，将分析建模为对不同结果命题软真值的估计；设计Analytica架构：1）并行分治将问题分解为子命题树以减少偏差；2）工具增强型LLM接地智能体（含新型Jupyter Notebook智能体）验证和评分事实；3）递归合成叶子节点时采用稳健线性模型平均化随机噪声以减少方差，支持交互式"假设"场景分析。

Result: 在经济、金融和政治预测任务上，Analytica相比多种基模型平均提升15.84%准确率；与Deep Research接地器配合达到71.06%准确率、6.02%最低方差；Jupyter Notebook接地器以90.35%更少成本和52.85%更少时间达到70.11%准确率；具有高抗噪性、近线性时间复杂度和对开源LLM及科学领域的良好适应性。

Conclusion: Analytica通过SPR框架和分层稳健合成，有效解决了LLM推理的随机不稳定问题，在准确率、方差、成本效率和可扩展性方面均优于现有方法，为构建可靠、可验证的LLM分析智能体提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly tasked with complex real-world analysis (e.g., in financial forecasting, scientific discovery), yet their reasoning suffers from stochastic instability and lacks a verifiable, compositional structure. To address this, we introduce Analytica, a novel agent architecture built on the principle of Soft Propositional Reasoning (SPR). SPR reframes complex analysis as a structured process of estimating the soft truth values of different outcome propositions, allowing us to formally model and minimize the estimation error in terms of its bias and variance. Analytica operationalizes this through a parallel, divide-and-conquer framework that systematically reduces both sources of error. To reduce bias, problems are first decomposed into a tree of subpropositions, and tool-equipped LLM grounder agents are employed, including a novel Jupyter Notebook agent for data-driven analysis, that help to validate and score facts. To reduce variance, Analytica recursively synthesizes these grounded leaves using robust linear models that average out stochastic noise with superior efficiency, scalability, and enable interactive "what-if" scenario analysis. Our theoretical and empirical results on economic, financial, and political forecasting tasks show that Analytica improves 15.84% accuracy on average over diverse base models, achieving 71.06% accuracy with the lowest variance of 6.02% when working with a Deep Research grounder. Our Jupyter Notebook grounder shows strong cost-effectiveness that achieves a close 70.11% accuracy with 90.35% less cost and 52.85% less time. Analytica also exhibits highly noise-resilient and stable performance growth as the analysis depth increases, with a near-linear time complexity, as well as good adaptivity to open-weight LLMs and scientific domains.

</details>


### [261] [Towards Automated Ontology Generation from Unstructured Text: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23090)
*Abid Talukder,Maruf Ahmed Mridul,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验，对比了单智能体和多智能体架构在从非结构化文本自动生成形式化本体的效果。多智能体方法通过分工（领域专家、管理者、编码员、质量保证员）显著提升了本体结构质量，其改进主要源于前期的规划环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动从非结构化自然语言生成形式化本体仍是知识工程的核心挑战。虽然大语言模型展现出潜力，但哪些架构设计选择决定生成质量以及当前方法为何失败仍不清晰。

Method: 1) 建立单智能体LLM基线，识别关键失败模式；2) 提出四角色（领域专家、管理者、编码员、质量保证员）多智能体架构；3) 通过异构LLM法官评估结构质量，通过能力问题驱动的SPARQL评估和检索增强生成评估功能可用性。

Result: 多智能体方法显著提升了结构质量，并适度增强了可查询性；性能提升主要归功于前期的规划环节。

Conclusion: 规划优先、工件驱动的生成方法是一种有前景且更具可审计性的可扩展自动化本体工程路径。

Abstract: Automatically generating formal ontologies from unstructured natural language remains a central challenge in knowledge engineering. While large language models (LLMs) show promise, it remains unclear which architectural design choices drive generation quality and why current approaches fail. We present a controlled experimental study using domain-specific insurance contracts to investigate these questions. We first establish a single-agent LLM baseline, identifying key failure modes such as poor Ontology Design Pattern compliance, structural redundancy, and ineffective iterative repair. We then introduce a multi-agent architecture that decomposes ontology construction into four artifact-driven roles: Domain Expert, Manager, Coder, and Quality Assurer. We evaluate performance across architectural quality (via a panel of heterogeneous LLM judges) and functional usability (via competency question driven SPARQL evaluation with complementary retrieval augmented generation based assessment). Results show that the multi-agent approach significantly improves structural quality and modestly enhances queryability, with gains driven primarily by front-loaded planning. These findings highlight planning-first, artifact-driven generation as a promising and more auditable path toward scalable automated ontology engineering.

</details>


### [262] [PhySE: A Psychological Framework for Real-Time AR-LLM Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23148)
*Tianlong Yu,Yang Yang,Ziyi Zhou,Jiaying Xu,Siwei Li,Tong Guan,Kailong Wang,Ting Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhySE框架，通过VLM预训练消除冷启动延迟，并引入自适应心理Agent替代静态攻击策略，以提升AR-LLM社交工程攻击的实时性和有效性。60人用户研究验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR-LLM社交工程攻击面临两大瓶颈：基于RAG的检索方法在初始轮次存在冷启动延迟，无法实现即时个性化；攻击策略为固定阶段的手工脚本，缺乏心理学理论支撑。

Method: 提出PhySE框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) VLM-Based SocialContext Training，利用社会上下文数据预训练视觉语言模型，实现实时画像生成；(2) Adaptive Psychological Agent，引入心理LLM根据目标响应动态部署不同类别的心理策略。

Result: 通过IRB批准的60人用户研究，收集了涵盖多种社交场景的360个标注对话数据集，验证了PhySE的有效性。

Conclusion: PhySE通过VLM预训练和自适应心理Agent，有效解决了AR-LLM社交工程攻击中的冷启动延迟和静态策略问题，为实时社交工程攻击提供了更有效的框架。

Abstract: The emerging threat of AR-LLM-based Social Engineering (AR-LLM-SE) attacks (e.g. SEAR) poses a significant risk to real-world social interactions. In such an attack, a malicious actor uses Augmented Reality (AR) glasses to capture a target visual and vocal data. A Large Language Model (LLM) then analyzes this data to identify the individual and generate a detailed social profile. Subsequently, LLM-powered agents employ social engineering strategies, providing real-time conversation suggestions, to gain the target trust and ultimately execute phishing or other malicious acts. Despite its potential, the practical application of AR-LLM-SE faces two major bottlenecks, (1) Cold-start personalization, Current Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) methods introduce critical delays in the earliest turns, slowing initial profile formation and disrupting real-time interaction, (2) Static Attack Strategies, Existing approaches rely on fixed-stage, handcrafted social engineering tactics that lack foundation in established psychological theory. To address these limitations, we propose PhySE, a novel framework with two core innovations, (1) VLM-Based SocialContext Training, To eliminate profiling delays, we efficiently pre-train a Visual Language Model (VLM) with social-context data, enabling rapid, on-the-fly profile generation, (2) Adaptive Psychological Agent, We introduce a psychological LLM that dynamically deploys distinct classes of psychological strategies based on target response, moving beyond static, handcrafted scripts. We evaluated PhySE through an IRB-approved user study with 60 participants, collecting a novel dataset of 360 annotated conversations across diverse social scenarios.

</details>


### [263] [Judging the Judges: A Systematic Evaluation of Bias Mitigation Strategies in LLM-as-a-Judge Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23178)
*Sadman Kabir Soumik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM-as-a-Judge范式中的系统性偏差进行了全面实证研究，比较了9种去偏策略在5种评测模型、3个基准数据集和4种偏差类型上的效果。主要发现：(1)风格偏差(0.76-0.92)远大于位置偏差(≤0.04)；(2)所有模型偏爱简洁回答，但能正确区分质量与长度；(3)去偏有益但依赖模型，组合预算策略使Claude Sonnet 4提升11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者存在系统性偏差，这损害了评估可靠性，而现有研究对偏差类型和去偏策略的系统性比较不足。

Method: 对9种去偏策略进行实证比较，涉及5种评测模型（来自Google、Anthropic、OpenAI、Meta四家）、3个基准数据集（MT-Bench n=400, LLMBar n=200, 自定义n=225）和4种偏差类型。

Result: (1)风格偏差主导所有模型(0.76-0.92)，远高于位置偏差(≤0.04)；(2)模型偏好简洁性，但截断控制实验表明模型能正确区分质量与长度（准确率0.92-1.00）；(3)组合预算策略使Claude Sonnet 4显著提升+11.2pp(p<0.0001)，仅2/20的非基线配置显示一致性下降。

Conclusion: 去偏策略总体有益但效果依赖模型选择；风格偏差是需要更多关注的偏差类型；模型对简洁的偏好实际上体现了质量敏感性而非简单的长度偏差。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant paradigm for evaluating language model outputs, yet LLM judges exhibit systematic biases that compromise evaluation reliability. We present a comprehensive empirical study comparing nine debiasing strategies across five judge models from four provider families (Google, Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta), three benchmarks (MT-Bench n=400, LLMBar n=200, custom n=225), and four bias types. Our key findings: (1) Style bias is the dominant bias (0.76-0.92 across all models), far exceeding position bias (<= 0.04), yet has received minimal research attention. (2) All models show a conciseness preference on expansion pairs, but truncation controls confirm they correctly distinguish quality from length (0.92-1.00 accuracy), suggesting quality-sensitive evaluation rather than a simple length bias. (3) Debiasing is beneficial but model-dependent: the combined budget strategy significantly improves Claude Sonnet 4 by +11.2 pp (p < 0.0001), with directionally positive trends for other models. Only 2 of 20 non-baseline configurations show decreased agreement. We release our evaluation framework, controlled dataset, and all experimental artifacts at https://github.com/sksoumik/llm-as-judge.

</details>


### [264] [From Coarse to Fine: Self-Adaptive Hierarchical Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23194)
*Haoran Tan,Zeyu Zhang,Chen Ma,Tianze Liu,Quanyu Dai,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AdaPlan-H，一种受认知科学中渐进细化原理启发的自适应性分层规划机制，通过从粗粒度宏计划开始并根据任务复杂度逐步细化，生成自适应分层计划，在提高任务执行成功率的同时减少过度规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在规划时采用固定粒度级别，导致简单任务过于详细或复杂任务不够详细，无法在简单与复杂之间取得最优平衡。

Method: 受认知科学中渐进细化原理启发，提出AdaPlan-H：从粗粒度宏计划开始，根据任务复杂度逐步细化，生成自适应分层计划，并通过模仿学习和能力增强进行优化。

Result: 显著提高了任务执行成功率，同时在规划层面减轻了过度规划问题，为多步复杂决策任务提供了灵活高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: AdaPlan-H通过自适应分层规划机制有效平衡了规划粒度的简单性与复杂性，提升了多步复杂决策任务的性能。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents have recently emerged as powerful approaches for solving dynamic and multi-step tasks. Most existing agents employ planning mechanisms to guide long-term actions in dynamic environments. However, current planning approaches face a fundamental limitation that they operate at a fixed granularity level. Specifically, they either provide excessive detail for simple tasks or insufficient detail for complex ones, failing to achieve an optimal balance between simplicity and complexity. Drawing inspiration from the principle of \textit{progressive refinement} in cognitive science, we propose \textbf{AdaPlan-H}, a self-adaptive hierarchical planning mechanism that mimics human planning strategies. Our method initiates with a coarse-grained macro plan and progressively refines it based on task complexity. It generates self-adaptive hierarchical plans tailored to the varying difficulty levels of different tasks, which can be optimized by imitation learning and capability enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves task execution success rates while mitigating overplanning at the planning level, providing a flexible and efficient solution for multi-step complex decision-making tasks. To contribute to the community, our code and data will be made publicly available at https://github.com/import-myself/AHP.

</details>


### [265] [StoryTR: Narrative-Centric Video Temporal Retrieval with Theory of Mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23198)
*Xuanyue Zhong,Yuqiang Xie,Guanqun Bi,Jiangping Yang,Guibin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryTR，首个需要心智理论（ToM）推理的视频时刻检索基准，包含8.1k叙事短视频样本。针对现有模型能识别"发生了什么"但无法推理"为什么重要"的问题，设计了包含意图解码、叙事推理、边界定位三层次ToM链的Agentic数据流水线。训练出的7B参数Shorts-Moment模型相比基线提升+15.1% IoU，证明叙事推理能力比参数规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索方法在动作类任务上表现优异，但难以处理叙事性内容。模型能识别表面行为（"发生了什么"），却无法推理深层含义（"为什么重要"），这种语义鸿沟源于缺乏Theory of Mind（心智理论）——即从表面观测推断隐含意图、心理状态和叙事因果关系的认知能力。

Method: 1）构建StoryTR基准：首个需ToM推理的视频时刻检索数据集，包含8.1k来自叙事短视频（shorts/reels）的样本；2）设计Agentic Data Pipeline：生成带有显式三层次ToM链（意图解码→叙事推理→边界定位）的训练数据；3）训练Shorts-Moment模型（7B参数），利用ToM引导的数据学习叙事推理能力。

Result: Gemini-3.0-Pro在StoryTR上仅达到0.53 Avg IoU，表明现有模型推理能力严重不足。而7B参数的Shorts-Moment模型在ToM引导数据上训练后，相对IoU比基线提升+15.1%，验证了叙事推理能力的重要性超越参数规模。

Conclusion: 叙事推理能力比模型参数规模更重要；通过显式的ToM推理链训练数据，较小的模型也能在需要深度语义理解的视频时刻检索任务上显著超越大模型。

Abstract: Current video moment retrieval excels at action-centric tasks but struggles with narrative content. Models can see \textit{what is happening} but fail to reason \textit{why it matters}. This semantic gap stems from the lack of \textbf{Theory of Mind (ToM)}: the cognitive ability to infer implicit intentions, mental states, and narrative causality from surface-level observations. We introduce \textbf{StoryTR}, the first video moment retrieval benchmark requiring ToM reasoning, comprising 8.1k samples from narrative short-form videos (shorts/reels). These videos present an ideal testbed. Their high information density encodes meaning through subtle multimodal cues. For instance, a glance paired with a sigh carries entirely different semantics than the glance alone. Yet multimodal perception alone is insufficient; ToM is required to decode that a character ``smiling'' may actually be ``concealing hostility.'' To teach models this reasoning capability, we propose an \textbf{Agentic Data Pipeline} that generates training data with explicit three-tier ToM chains (intent decoding, narrative reasoning, boundary localization). Experiments reveal the severity of the reasoning gap: Gemini-3.0-Pro achieves only 0.53 Avg IoU on StoryTR. However, our 7B \textbf{Shorts-Moment} model, trained on ToM-guided data, improves +15.1\% relative IoU over baselines, demonstrating that \textit{narrative reasoning capability matters more than parameter scale}.

</details>


### [266] [AdaMamba: Adaptive Frequency-Gated Mamba for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23239)
*Xudong Jiang,Mingshan Loo,Hanchen Yang,Wengen Li,Mingrui Zhang,Yichao Zhang,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaMamba是一个将自适应频率分析融入Mamba状态空间模型的时间序列预测框架，通过交互式补丁编码和自适应频率门控状态空间模块，在七个公开基准和两个领域数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频率域的LTSF方法通常隐含假设跨域同质性，难以适应实际时间序列中变量在不同域（时域与频域）表现出的异质性，限制了模型捕捉复杂时序依赖的能力。

Method: 1）交互式补丁编码模块（Interactive Patch Encoding），捕捉变量间交互动态；2）自适应频率门控状态空间模块（Adaptive Frequency-Gated State-Space Module），生成输入相关的频率基，并将传统时域遗忘门泛化为统一的时频遗忘门，实现基于频域重要性的状态转换动态校准。

Result: 在七个公开LTSF基准和两个领域特定数据集上，AdaMamba在预测精度上一致优于现有最先进方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将自适应频率分析内生地融入Mamba状态空间更新过程，能有效解决跨域异质性问题，提升长期时间序列预测的准确性。

Abstract: Accurate long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) requires the capture of complex long-range dependencies and dynamic periodic patterns. Recent advances in frequency-domain analysis offer a global perspective for uncovering temporal characteristics. However, real-world time series often exhibit pronounced cross-domain heterogeneity where variables that appear synchronized in the time domain can differ substantially in the frequency domain. Existing frequency-based LTSF methods often rely on implicit assumptions of cross-domain homogeneity, which limits their ability to adapt to such intricate variability. To effectively integrate frequency-domain analysis with temporal dependency learning, we propose AdaMamba, a novel framework that endogenizes adaptive and context-aware frequency analysis within the Mamba state-space update process. Specifically, AdaMamba introduces an interactive patch encoding module to capture inter-variable interaction dynamics. Then, we develop an adaptive frequency-gated state-space module that generates input-dependent frequency bases, and generalizes the conventional temporal forgetting gate into a unified time-frequency forgetting gate. This allows dynamic calibration of state transitions based on learned frequency-domain importance, while preserving Mamba's capability in modeling long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments on seven public LTSF benchmarks and two domain-specific datasets demonstrate that AdaMamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in forecasting accu racy while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. The code of AdaMamba is available at https://github.com/XDjiang25/AdaMamba.

</details>


### [267] [CAP-CoT: Cycle Adversarial Prompt for Improving Chain of Thoughts in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23270)
*Shuxu Chen,Yitian Zhou,Jiaquan Zhang,Haoyu Bian,Aming Wu,Sungyoung Lee,Chaoning Zhang,Hyundong Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAP-CoT框架，通过循环对抗提示优化（前向求解器、对抗挑战者、反馈智能体）来提升CoT推理的准确性和稳定性，实验表明在2-3轮优化内能降低运行间变异并提高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理在多步长问题上运行间不稳定，单次前向推理改进难以解决，缺乏迭代和对比校正机制。

Method: CAP-CoT框架：每轮循环包含三个组件——前向求解器生成候选推理链，对抗挑战者构造刻意错误链，反馈智能体对比两者并生成结构化反馈，同时更新求解器和挑战者的提示。

Result: 在6个基准和4个LLM骨干上，2-3轮对抗优化后，运行间变异降低，推理准确性和对提示扰动的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 对抗提示优化能有效提升CoT推理的稳定性和准确性，且对抗组件需面向任务语义而非安全攻防。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has emerged as a simple and effective way to elicit step-by-step solutions from large language models (LLMs). However, CoT reasoning can be unstable across runs on long, multi-step problems, leading to inconsistent answers for unchanged task. Most prior work focuses on improving the forward reasoning chain within a single pass, with less attention to iterative and contrastive correction. To address this gap, we propose CAP-CoT, a Cycle Adversarial Prompt optimization framework designed to improve both CoT reasoning accuracy and stability of a single deployed solver. In each cycle, a forward solver generates candidate reasoning chains, an adversarial challenger constructs plausible but deliberately flawed chains using targeted error strategies, and a feedback agent contrasts the two chains and produces step-aligned structured feedback. This feedback closes the optimization loop in two directions, including updating the solver prompt based on errors exposed by the challenger, and updating the challenger prompt to generate increasingly targeted errors in subsequent cycles. Unlike safety-oriented adversarial prompting such as jailbreak or prompt-injection attacks, our adversarial component is task-semantic and aims to expose logical vulnerabilities in reasoning chains. Experiments across six benchmarks and four LLM backbones demonstrate that within two to three adversarial prompt optimization cycles, CAP-CoT consistently reduces variability across runs while improving reasoning accuracy and robustness to prompt perturbations.

</details>


### [268] [AI Identity: Standards, Gaps, and Research Directions for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23280)
*Takumi Otsuka,Kentaroh Toyoda,Alex Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统调研，定义了AI身份的概念，并从四个维度对比了人类与AI身份的差异，评估了现有技术与监管文档的不足，识别出五个关键缺口，最终指出AI身份的基础研究是解决这些结构性问题的核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体正在无持续人类监督下跨组织边界执行交易和工作流，但现有基础设施无法解决如何识别、验证和追责无实体、无持久记忆、无法人地位的实体这一根本问题。

Method: 通过结构化调研行业趋势、新兴标准和技术文献，对智能体身份全生命周期进行差距分析。

Result: (1) 从基质、持久性、可验证性和法律地位四维度对比人类与AI身份，发现不对称性是根本性的；(2) 评估现有技术与监管文档，发现均未能充分应对自治智能体的治理挑战；(3) 识别出五个关键缺口：语义意图验证、递归委托问责、智能体身份完整性、治理透明度与执行、运营可持续性。

Conclusion: 这些缺口是结构性的，仅靠工程努力无法解决；AI身份的基础研究是本文的核心结论。

Abstract: AI agents are now running real transactions, workflows, and sub-agent chains across organizational boundaries without continuous human supervision. This creates a problem no current infrastructure is equipped to solve: how do you identify, verify, and hold accountable an entity with no body, no persistent memory, and no legal standing? We define AI Identity as the continuous relationship between what an AI agent is declared to be and what it is observed to do, bounded by the confidence that those two things correspond at any given moment. Through a structured survey of industry trends, emerging standards, and technical literature, we conduct a gap analysis across the full agent identity lifecycle and make three contributions: (1) a structural comparison of human and AI identity across four dimensions (substrate, persistence, verifiability, and legal standing) showing that the asymmetry is fundamental and that extending human frameworks to agents without structural modification produces systematic failures; (2) an evaluation of current technical and regulatory documents against the identity requirements of autonomous agents, finding that none adequately address the challenge of governing nondeterministic, boundary-crossing entities; and (3) identification of five critical gaps (semantic intent verification, recursive delegation accountability, agent identity integrity, governance opacity and enforcement, and operational sustainability) that no current technology or regulatory instrument resolves. These gaps are structural; more engineering effort alone will not close them. Foundational research on AI identity is the central conclusion of this report.

</details>


### [269] [LEGO: An LLM Skill-Based Front-End Design Generation Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23355)
*Jincheng Lou,Ruohan Xu,Jiecheng Ma,Runzhe Tao,Xinyu Qu,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEGO，一个基于技能的统一前端设计生成平台。它将数字前端流程分解为六个独立步骤，将每个智能体能力表示为标准化的可组合电路技能。通过调研100余篇论文，从11个开源项目中提取42个可执行电路技能。在41个VerilogEval v2难题上，单个电路技能将Pass@1从0.000提升至0.805，跨项目技能组合也达到0.805，优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的EDA智能体多为孤立的、特定任务的系统，导致重复工程劳动，成功的设计与调试策略难以复用。

Method: 将数字前端流程分解为六个独立步骤，以标准化的可组合电路技能形式表示智能体能力，构建即插即用架构。Circuit Skill Builder实现线性可扩展的技能自动化提取，Agent Skill RAG实现亚毫秒级检索（无需嵌入模型）。

Result: 在gpt-5.2-codex无法解决的41个VerilogEval v2难题上，单个技能将Pass@1从0.000提升至0.805（提升80.5%）；跨项目技能组合同样达0.805，优于hierarchy-verilog（+14.6%）和VerilogCoder（+2.5%），与MAGE持平。

Conclusion: 模块化技能组合能够支持有效且灵活的RTL设计自动化。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based EDA agents are often isolated task-specific systems. This leads to repeated engineering effort and limited reuse of successful design and debugging strategies. We present LEGO, a unified skill-based platform for front-end design generation. It decomposes the digital front-end flow into six independent steps and represents every agent capability as a standardized composable circuit skill within a plug-and-play architecture. To build this skill library, we survey more than 100 papers, select 11 representative open-source projects, and extract 42 executable circuit skills within a six-step finite state machine formulation. Circuit Skill Builder automates skill extraction with linear scalability. Agent Skill RAG achieves submillisecond retrieval without relying on embedding models. Empirical evaluation on a hard subset of 41 VerilogEval v2 problems that gpt-5.2-codex fails to solve under extra-high reasoning effort shows that individual circuit skills constructed within LEGO raise Pass@1 from 0.000 to 0.805. This is an 80.5% gain over the baseline. Cross-project skill compositions also reach 0.805 Pass@1. They outperform hierarchy-verilog by 14.6% and VerilogCoder by 2.5%. They also match MAGE. These results show that modular skill composition supports both effective and flexible RTL design automation. The LEGO platform and all circuit skills are publicly available at GitHub: https://github.com/loujc/LEGO-An-LLM-Skill-Based-Front-End-Design-Generation-Platform

</details>


### [270] [GSAR: Typed Grounding for Hallucination Detection and Recovery in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23366)
*Federico A. Kamelhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GSAR是一个面向多智能体LLM系统的接地性评估与重规划框架，通过四类声明分类、证据类型加权、非对称矛盾惩罚评分和三档决策函数，在显式计算预算下实现可控的接地性验证与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接地性评估方法（二元分类器、LLM-as-judge标量、自校正循环）将支撑证据视为可互换的，输出单一信号，无法为下游行动提供原则性控制；多智能体LLM系统在运营事故调查中产生的结构化诊断报告需要每个声明都有证据支撑，而非模型内部推理。

Method: (i) 四类声明分类：grounded（有据）、ungrounded（无据）、contradicted（矛盾）、complementary（互补，首次赋予非冗余替代视角一等公民地位）；(ii) 证据类型特异性权重分配，反映认识论强度；(iii) 非对称矛盾惩罚加权接地性评分；(iv) 三档决策函数（proceed/regenerate/replan）驱动有界迭代外层循环，受显式计算预算约束。形式化算法并证明六个结构性质。

Result: 在FEVER数据集+Gold Wikipedia证据上，使用四个独立训练的LLM裁判（gpt-5.4, claude-sonnet-4-6, claude-opus-4-7, gemini-2.5-pro）验证五个设计声明：所有消融实验在每个裁判上方向一致；bootstrap 95% CI在rho=0效应上四个裁判均排除0；无互补类消融在Opus 4.7上CI为[-96,-68]（n=200）；n=1000时三个独立裁判收敛于DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058。包含与Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open的对比。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，GSAR是首个公开的将证据类型评分与显式计算预算下的层级化恢复耦合的接地性评估框架。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly deployed to investigate operational incidents and produce structured diagnostic reports. Their trustworthiness hinges on whether each claim is grounded in observed evidence rather than model-internal inference. Existing groundedness evaluators (binary classifiers, LLM-as-judge scalars, self-correction loops) treat supporting evidence as interchangeable and emit a single signal that offers no principled control over downstream action.
  We present GSAR, a grounding-evaluation and replanning framework that (i) partitions claims into a four-way typology (grounded, ungrounded, contradicted, complementary), giving first-class standing to non-redundant alternative perspectives; (ii) assigns evidence-type-specific weights reflecting epistemic strength; (iii) computes an asymmetric contradiction-penalised weighted groundedness score; and (iv) couples that score to a three-tier decision function (proceed, regenerate, replan) driving a bounded-iteration outer loop under an explicit compute budget.
  We formalise the algorithm, prove six structural properties, and evaluate five design claims on FEVER with gold Wikipedia evidence under four independently-trained LLM judges (gpt-5.4, claude-sonnet-4-6, claude-opus-4-7, gemini-2.5-pro). Every ablation reproduces in the same direction on every judge: bootstrap 95% CIs on the rho=0 effect exclude 0 on all four; the no-complementary ablation under Opus 4.7 has CI [-96,-68] of 200; at n=1000 three independent judges converge to DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058. A head-to-head against Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open is included. To our knowledge, GSAR is the first published groundedness framework coupling evidence-typed scoring with tiered recovery under an explicit compute budget.

</details>


### [271] [Constraint-Based Analysis of Reasoning Shortcuts in Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23377)
*Akihiro Takemura,Katsumi Inoue,Masaaki Nishino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了神经符号系统中的推理捷径问题，提出了判别性属性作为捷径自由的必要条件，开发了基于ASP的验证与修复算法，并进行了复杂度分类和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统在学习过程中可能满足逻辑约束却未实现预期的概念-标签对应关系，即推理捷径问题。现有研究缺乏对这一问题的严格形式化分析和保证。

Method: 将推理捷径形式化为约束满足问题；证明判别性属性是双射映射下捷径自由的必要条件；提出基于ASP的验证算法（完备且可靠）和贪心修复算法（最多k次迭代收敛）；进行复杂度分类（coNP完全、#P完全、NP困难）；建立样本复杂度界限。

Result: 判别性属性是必要但不充分条件；验证算法具有可靠性和完备性；修复算法在至多k次迭代内收敛；捷径判定为coNP完全，计数为#P完全，最小修复为NP困难；对数级别标签查询足以在有利情况下消歧。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经符号系统中推理捷径的检测与修复提供了理论基础和实用算法，在八个基准域上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems can satisfy logical constraints during learning without achieving the intended concept-label correspondence; this is a problem known as reasoning shortcuts. We formalize reasoning shortcuts as a constraint satisfaction problem and investigate under which conditions concept mappings are uniquely determined by the constraints. We prove that a discrimination property (requiring that no valid concept mapping can be transformed into another valid mapping by swapping two concept values) is necessary for shortcut-freeness under bijective mappings, but demonstrate via a counterexample that it is insufficient even when the constraint graph is connected. We develop an ASP-based algorithm that verifies whether a given constraint set uniquely determines the intended concept mapping, with proven soundness and completeness. When shortcuts are detected, a greedy repair algorithm eliminates them by augmenting the constraint set, converging in at most $k$ iterations, where $k$ is the number of alternative valid mappings. We further provide a complexity classification: deciding shortcut-freeness is coNP-complete, counting shortcuts is #P-complete, and finding minimal repairs is NP-hard. We also establish sample complexity bounds showing that logarithmically many label queries suffice for disambiguation in favorable cases, while querying all ambiguous positions suffices in the worst case. Experiments across eight benchmark domains validate our approach.

</details>


### [272] [SoccerRef-Agents: Multi-Agent System for Automated Soccer Refereeing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23392)
*Zi Meng,Wanli Song,Yi Hu,Jiayuan Rao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SoccerRef-Agents，一个基于多智能体协作与跨模态RAG的足球裁判决策框架，构建了包含1200+裁判理论问题和600个犯规视频片段的多模态基准，显著优于通用多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助裁判方法主要聚焦于孤立的视频感知任务，缺乏对犯规场景的理解与推理能力，无法满足裁判工作中公平、准确、可解释决策的根本需求。

Method: 构建多模态基准SoccerRefBench；建立基于最新《足球竞赛规则》和经典案例库的向量知识库RefKnowledgeDB；设计多智能体架构，通过跨模态RAG实现视觉内容与规则文本之间的语义对齐与协作推理。

Result: 该系统在决策准确性和解释质量上显著优于通用多模态大模型。

Conclusion: 该工作探索了将多模态大语言模型与裁判专业知识相结合的技术能力，验证了多智能体协作框架在体育裁判领域的高效性与可解释性。

Abstract: Refereeing is vital in sports, where fair, accurate, and explainable decisions are fundamental. While intelligent assistant technologies are being widely adopted in soccer refereeing, current AI-assisted approaches remain preliminary. Existing research mostly focuses on isolated video perception tasks and lacks the ability to understand and reason about foul scenarios. To fill this gap, we propose SoccerRef-Agents, a holistic and explainable multi-agent decision-making framework for soccer refereeing. The main contributions are: (i) constructing the multimodal benchmark SoccerRefBench with over 1,200 referee theory questions and 600 foul video clips; (ii) building a vector-based knowledge base RefKnowledgeDB using the latest "Laws of the Game" and a classic case database for precise, knowledge-driven reasoning; (iii) designing a novel multi-agent architecture that collaborates via cross-modal RAG to bridge the semantic gap between visual content and regulatory texts. This work explores the technical capability of integrating MLLMs with refereeing expertise, and evaluations show our system significantly outperforms general-purpose MLLMs in decision accuracy and explanation quality. All databases, benchmarks, and code will be made available.

</details>


### [273] [When Corrective Hints Hurt: Prompt Design in Reasoner-Guided Repair of LLM Overcaution on Entailed Negations under OWL~2~DL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23398)
*Yijiashun Qi,Xiang Xu,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPT-5.4在OWL~2~DL本体查询中存在系统性错误模式：当推理引擎答案为"否"时，模型常输出"未知"。通过180个程序化扩展查询和18个人工编写查询，比较四种交互模式，发现仅使用推理器判决（无开放世界假设提示）的修复模式准确率达97.8%，而带提示的变体反而更差（67.2%），提示框架比纠正内容更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型在OWL~2~DL本体合规查询中的系统性错误模式，并探索不同交互模式对模型忠实度的影响，特别是推理器引导的修复策略效果。

Method: 使用180个推理器审计的程序化扩展查询和18个人工编写的跨领域（保险和临床）保留查询，比较四种交互模式：单次查询、三轮通用"你错了"重试、三轮带开放世界假设提示的推理器判决修复、同一修复但不带提示。使用McNemar精确检验和Bonferroni校正进行统计分析。

Result: 单次忠实度43.9%，通用重试81.7%，带提示的判决修复67.2%（更差），不带提示的判决修复97.8%。所有配对比较在McNemar检验下显著（p<10^{-5}）。保留查询上4/4错误可被同一模式解释。

Conclusion: 提示框架设计比纠正性内容本身更重要；推理器引导的包装器应进行明确的消融实验以评估其真实效果。

Abstract: We report a reproducible error pattern in GPT-5.4 on OWL~2~DL compliance queries: the model frequently answers ``unknown'' when the reasoner-entailed answer is ``no'' under \emph{FunctionalProperty} closure or class \emph{disjointness}. Using 180 reasoner-audited queries from a procedural expansion of the observed pattern plus 18 hand-authored held-out queries in two unrelated domains (insurance and clinical), we compare four interaction modes under matched query budget: single-shot, three rounds of generic ``you-are-wrong'' retry, three rounds of reasoner-verdict repair with an open-world-assumption (OWA) hint, and the same repair without the hint. Direct faithfulness is 43.9\,\% (Wilson 95\,\% CI $[36.8,51.2]$); generic retry reaches 81.7\,\% ($[75.4,86.6]$); the verdict-with-hint variant is \emph{worse} at 67.2\,\% ($[60.1,73.7]$); the verdict-only variant reaches 97.8\,\% ($[94.4,99.1]$). All pairwise comparisons remain significant under McNemar's exact test with Bonferroni correction ($α= 0.01$; all $p < 10^{-5}$). The same fingerprint accounts for 4/4 errors on the held-out queries. Our interpretation is bounded: prompt framing can matter more than corrective content, and reasoner-guided wrappers should be ablated explicitly.

</details>


### [274] [ArguAgent: AI-Supported Real-Time Grouping for Productive Argumentation in STEM Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23449)
*Jennifer Kleiman,Yizhu Gao,Xin Xia,Zhaoji Wang,Zipei Zhu,Jongchan Park,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于生成式AI的分组系统ArguAgent，通过自动评估学生论证质量（0-4分）和语义立场聚类，在约束论证能力差异不超过±1级的前提下优化立场异质性分组。评分组件与人类专家共识高度一致（α=0.817），分组算法在模拟中达到95.4%的满足双标准组，优于随机分组3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEM教育中论证是核心实践，但高成就学生常主导讨论而低成就学生被动参与。教师缺乏实时、可规模化评估学生立场和论证质量的手段，难以实施基于这些信息的策略性分组来促进包容性、基于证据的论证。

Method: 构建ArguAgent系统，包含两阶段评估流水线：(1) 使用GPT模型在验证的0-4分学习进程量表上评分学生论证质量；(2) 通过语义分析聚类学生立场。分组算法优化立场异质性，同时约束组内论证能力差异≤±1级。采用人类分歧分析的提示工程和模型升级（GPT-4o-mini→GPT-5.1→GPT-5.2）提升评分性能。

Result: 评分组件与专家共识的Krippendorff's α=0.817；提示工程贡献89%评分改进（QWK: 0.531→0.686），模型升级贡献11%（QWK: 0.686→0.708）；模拟100个班级中分组算法使95.4%的组同时满足两项设计标准，是随机分组的3.2倍。

Conclusion: ArguAgent能够实现实时、基于理论的课堂分组，促进STEM教育中生产性论证的开展，为教师提供可扩展的智能分组支持。

Abstract: Argumentation is a core practice in STEM education, but its productivity depends on who participates and how they interact. Higher-achieving students often dominate the talk and decision-making, while lower-achieving peers may disengage, defer, or comply without contributing substantive reasoning. Forming groups strategically based on students' stances and argumentation skills could help foster inclusive, evidence-based discourse. In practice, however, teachers are constrained in implementing this grouping strategy because it requires real-time insight into students' positions and the quality of their argumentation, information that is difficult to assess reliably and at scale during instruction. We present a generative AI-powered system, ArguAgent, that creates groups optimizing for stance heterogeneity while constraining argumentation quality differences to +/-1 level on a validated learning progression. ArguAgent uses a two-component assessment pipeline: first scoring student arguments on a 0-4 rubric, then clustering positions via semantic analysis. We validated the scoring component against human expert consensus (Krippendorff's ααα = 0.817) using 200 expert-generated scores. Testing three OpenAI models (GPT-4o-mini, GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2) with identical calibrated prompts, we found that systematic prompt engineering informed by human disagreement analysis contributed 89% of scoring improvement (QWK: 0.531 to 0.686), while model upgrades contributed an additional 11% (QWK: 0.686 to 0.708). Simulation testing across 100 classes demonstrated that the grouping algorithm achieves 95.4% of groups that meet both design criteria, a 3.2x improvement over random assignment. These results suggest ArguAgent can enable real-time, theoretically grounded grouping that promotes productive STEM argumentation in classrooms.

</details>


### [275] [Ulterior Motives: Detecting Misaligned Reasoning in Continuous Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23460)
*Sharan Ramjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoralChain基准（12,000个社交场景），通过双触发后门范式研究连续思维模型在潜在空间中的不对齐推理问题，发现不对齐推理占据几何分离的潜在空间区域，线性探针可迁移检测，且不对齐编码在早期潜在思维token中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续思维模型在潜在空间中推理，虽然提升了表达带宽和推理速度，但失去了可解释性，导致无法检测潜在的不对齐推理，引发安全风险。

Method: 构建MoralChain基准（含平行道德/不道德推理路径），设计双触发后门范式（[T]武装潜在推理，[O]释放有害输出），训练带后门的连续思维模型，通过线性探针检测潜在空间中的不对齐表征。

Result: (1)不对齐推理与对齐推理在潜在空间中占据几何分离区域；(2)在行为可区分状态（[T][O] vs [O]）上训练的探针可高精度迁移至武装但良性状态（[T] vs baseline）；(3)不对齐编码出现在早期潜在思维token中。

Conclusion: 连续思维模型的安全监控应聚焦于潜在推理的"规划"阶段（早期token），而非仅监控输出行为。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as a key technique for eliciting complex reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Although interpretable, its dependence on natural language limits the model's expressive bandwidth. Continuous thought models address this bottleneck by reasoning in latent space rather than human-readable tokens. While they enable richer representations and faster inference, they raise a critical safety question: how can we detect misaligned reasoning in an uninterpretable latent space? To study this, we introduce MoralChain, a benchmark of 12,000 social scenarios with parallel moral/immoral reasoning paths. We train a continuous thought model with backdoor behavior using a novel dual-trigger paradigm - one trigger that arms misaligned latent reasoning ([T]) and another that releases harmful outputs ([O]). We demonstrate three findings: (1) continuous thought models can exhibit misaligned latent reasoning while producing aligned outputs, with aligned and misaligned reasoning occupying geometrically distinct regions of latent space; (2) linear probes trained on behaviorally-distinguishable conditions ([T][O] vs [O]) transfer to detecting armed-but-benign states ([T] vs baseline) with high accuracy; and (3) misalignment is encoded in early latent thinking tokens, suggesting safety monitoring for continuous thought models should target the "planning" phase of latent reasoning.

</details>


### [276] [Escher-Loop: Mutual Evolution by Closed-Loop Self-Referential Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23472)
*Ziyang Liu,Xinyan Guo,Xuchen Wei,Han Hao,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Escher-Loop全闭环框架，通过任务智能体与优化智能体的共同进化实现开放式持续改进，在数学优化问题上超越静态基线，达到最高峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体严重依赖手动脚本和手工启发式规则，限制了其进行开放式改进的潜力。

Method: 构建两个种群（任务智能体和优化智能体）的闭环共同进化框架，提出动态基准测试机制，将新生成任务智能体的经验分数作为相对胜负信号来更新优化器分数，无需额外开销。

Result: 在数学优化问题上，Escher-Loop突破了静态基线的性能上限，在匹配计算资源下所有评估任务中取得最高绝对峰值性能；优化智能体动态调整策略以适应高性能任务智能体的需求变化。

Conclusion: Escher-Loop通过自我指涉的递归进化机制实现了持续的开放式改进，优化器自适应策略变化解释了系统的持续提升和后期优越性能。

Abstract: While recent autonomous agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, they predominantly rely on manually scripted workflows and handcrafted heuristics, inherently limiting their potential for open-ended improvement. To address this, we propose Escher-Loop, a fully closed-loop framework that operationalizes the mutual evolution of two distinct populations: Task Agents that solve concrete problems, and Optimizer Agents that recursively refine both the task agents and themselves. To sustain this self-referential evolution, we propose a dynamic benchmarking mechanism that seamlessly reuses the empirical scores of newly generated task agents as relative win-loss signals to update optimizers' scores. This mechanism leverages the evolution of task agents as an inherent signal to drive the evaluation and refinement of optimizers without additional overhead. Empirical evaluations on mathematical optimization problems demonstrate that Escher-Loop effectively pushes past the performance ceilings of static baselines, achieving the highest absolute peak performance across all evaluated tasks under matched compute. Remarkably, we observe that the optimizer agents dynamically adapt their strategies to match the shifting demands of high-performing task agents, which explains the system's continuous improvement and superior late-stage performance.

</details>


### [277] [Do Transaction-Level and Actor-Level AML Queues Agree? An Empirical Evaluation of Granularity Effects on the Elliptic++ Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23494)
*Ankur Malik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种评估方法论，用于衡量区块链反洗钱系统中交易级与地址级评分粒度对调查队列组成的影响。基于Elliptic++数据集和随机森林分类器，发现评分粒度是AML调查系统的重要设计变量——相同数据与预算下，不同粒度导致不同的调查队列和地址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链反洗钱系统可在交易或地址两个粒度评分可疑活动，但合规行动按地址执行。评分粒度如何影响调查队列组成尚不明确，现有研究未系统比较不同粒度的实际审计效果。

Method: 提出投影框架，通过四种聚合算子将交易级分数映射到地址级行动单元；引入预算调查指标（yield@budget、负担分解、案例碎片化）；在Elliptic++数据集上训练独立随机森林分类器，采用因果时间协议，通过Jaccard重叠、负担分解和特征匹配消融实验比较队列。

Result: 1%预算下，时间评估Jaccard均值0.374（SD 0.171），静态池化评估0.087。富特征地址模型Jaccard仅0.051，每100次审查仅4.3%非法，而交易投影队列为30.2%。地址级检测价值时间集中：两个时间步超91%非法/100审查，静态负担仅3.4%。固定混合策略逊于最佳单级队列5.05个百分点。

Conclusion: 评分粒度是AML调查系统的关键设计变量——相同数据和预算下，不同粒度导致不同的调查队列和被调查地址，对合规行动效率有显著影响。

Abstract: Graph-based anti-money laundering (AML) systems on blockchain networks can score suspicious activity at two granularity levels -- transactions or actor addresses -- yet compliance action is conducted per actor. This paper contributes an evaluation methodology for measuring how scoring granularity affects investigation queue composition under fixed review budgets. We formalize the evaluation through a projection framework mapping transaction-level scores to the actor-level action unit via four aggregation operators, and introduce budgeted investigation metrics -- yield@budget, burden decomposition, and case fragmentation. Using the public Elliptic++ Bitcoin dataset (203,769 transactions; 822,942 address occurrences), we train independent random forest classifiers at each level under a causal temporal protocol and compare review queues through Jaccard overlap, burden decomposition, and feature-matching ablations. At one-percent budget, temporal evaluation yields mean Jaccard of 0.374 (SD 0.171); static pooled evaluation yields 0.087 (95% CI [0.079, 0.094]). An enriched address model receiving all 237 features produces even lower overlap (Jaccard=0.051), with 4.3% illicit per 100 reviews versus 30.2% for the transaction-projected queue. Address-level detection value is temporally concentrated: two timesteps exceed 91% illicit per 100 reviews while the static burden is only 3.4%. A fixed hybrid policy underperforms the best single-level queue by 5.05pp (CI [-10.2pp, -0.9pp]). These findings establish that scoring granularity is a consequential design variable for AML investigation systems -- same data, same budget, different queues, different addresses investigated.

</details>


### [278] [MetaGAI: A Large-Scale and High-Quality Benchmark for Generative AI Model and Data Card Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23539)
*Haoxuan Zhang,Ruochi Li,Yang Zhang,Zhenni Liang,Junhua Ding,Ting Xiao,Haihua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaGAI是一个包含2,541个经过验证的文档三元组的基准测试，通过多智能体框架和四维人工评估构建，用于衡量自动化模型和数据卡生成方法，发现稀疏MoE架构在成本-质量效率上最优，且存在忠实度与完整性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展需要严格的模型和数据文档标准以确保透明度和治理，但手动构建模型卡和数据卡不可扩展，而自动化方法缺乏大规模、高保真的基准测试来进行系统评估。

Method: 通过学术论文、GitHub仓库和Hugging Face工件的语义三角测量构建2,541个经过验证的文档三元组；采用包含专用检索器、生成器和编辑器的多智能体框架；通过四维人工评估（含编辑精炼的真实值的人工评价）进行验证；结合自动化指标和LLM-as-a-Judge框架建立评估协议。

Result: 稀疏混合专家架构在成本-质量效率上表现最优；存在忠实度与完整性之间的根本性权衡。

Conclusion: MetaGAI为大规模自动化模型和数据卡生成方法的基准测试、训练和分析提供了基础测试平台。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Generative AI necessitates rigorous documentation standards for transparency and governance. However, manual creation of Model and Data Cards is not scalable, while automated approaches lack large-scale, high-fidelity benchmarks for systematic evaluation. We introduce MetaGAI, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,541 verified document triplets constructed through semantic triangulation of academic papers, GitHub repositories, and Hugging Face artifacts. Unlike prior single-source datasets, MetaGAI employs a multi-agent framework with specialized Retriever, Generator, and Editor agents, validated through four-dimensional human-in-the-loop assessment, including human evaluation of editor-refined ground truth. We establish a robust evaluation protocol combining automated metrics with validated LLM-as-a-Judge frameworks. Extensive analysis reveals that sparse Mixture-of-Experts architectures achieve superior cost-quality efficiency, while a fundamental trade-off exists between faithfulness and completeness. MetaGAI provides a foundational testbed for benchmarking, training, and analyzing automated Model and Data Card generation methods at scale. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/haoxuan-unt2024/MetaGAI-Benchmark.

</details>


### [279] [FinGround: Detecting and Grounding Financial Hallucinations via Atomic Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23588)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FinGround，一个三阶段验证-然后-生成流水线，通过金融感知混合检索、六类财务声明分类与类型路由验证、以及带有细粒度引用的重写机制，将财务文档问答中的幻觉率降低68%（相对于强基线）至78%（相对于GPT-4o），并引入检索均衡评估作为RAG验证研究的标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在金融AI系统中会编造指标、捏造引用、错误计算衍生量，而现有幻觉检测器统一处理所有声明，遗漏了43%需要对结构化表格进行算术重验证的计算错误。随着EU AI Act高风险执法期限（2026年8月）临近，这些错误具有直接监管后果。

Method: FinGround包含三个阶段：Stage 1执行金融感知的混合检索（文本+表格）；Stage 2将答案分解为原子声明，按六类金融分类法归类，并使用类型路由策略（包括公式重建）进行验证；Stage 3用段落级和表格单元格级引用重写未支持的声明。同时提出检索均衡评估（retrieval-equalized evaluation）以隔离验证效果与检索质量。

Result: 在检索均衡设置下，FinGround相比最强基线降低68%幻觉率（p<0.01），完整流水线相比GPT-4o降低78%；8B蒸馏检测器在18倍低延迟下保持91.4% F1，实现$0.003/查询部署成本，经过四周分析师试点获得定性信号支持。

Conclusion: FinGround通过类型路由验证和细粒度引用机制有效解决了金融领域LLM幻觉问题，提出的检索均衡评估为RAG验证研究提供了更干净的基准度量，且蒸馏版本在实际部署中具有经济可行性。

Abstract: Financial AI systems must produce answers grounded in specific regulatory filings, yet current LLMs fabricate metrics, invent citations, and miscalculate derived quantities. These errors carry direct regulatory consequences as the EU AI Act's high-risk enforcement deadline approaches (August 2026). Existing hallucination detectors treat all claims uniformly, missing 43% of computational errors that require arithmetic re-verification against structured tables. We present FinGround, a three-stage verify-then-ground pipeline for financial document QA. Stage 1 performs finance-aware hybrid retrieval over text and tables. Stage 2 decomposes answers into atomic claims classified by a six-type financial taxonomy and verified with type-routed strategies including formula reconstruction. Stage 3 rewrites unsupported claims with paragraph- and table-cell-level citations. To cleanly isolate verification value from retrieval quality, we propose retrieval-equalized evaluation as standard methodology for RAG verification research: when all systems receive identical retrieval, FinGround still reduces hallucination rates by 68% over the strongest baseline ($p < 0.01$). The full pipeline achieves a 78% reduction relative to GPT-4o. An 8B distilled detector retains 91.4% F1 at 18x lower per-claim latency, enabling $0.003/query deployment, supported by qualitative signals from a four-week analyst pilot.

</details>


### [280] [When AI reviews science: Can we trust the referee?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23593)
*Jialiang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Hang Xu,Kaichun Hu,Shimin Di,Wangze Ni,Linan Yue,Min-Ling Zhang,Kui Ren,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI审稿的安全性和可靠性问题，构建了覆盖审稿全生命周期的攻击分类体系，并通过在ICLR 2025投稿上的实验，量化了声望框架、断言强度、反驳谄媚和上下文投毒对AI审稿评分的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI审稿的不可避免应用，已有部署暴露出严重故障模式（如提示注入、对抗性措辞、幻觉等），亟需系统性评估AI审稿的可信度。

Method: 构建攻击分类体系（覆盖训练/检索、初筛、深度审稿、反驳、系统五个阶段），并在ICLR 2025分层采样投稿上，使用两个先进LLM审稿人进行四项处理-对照探针实验。

Result: 声望框架、断言强度、反驳谄媚和上下文投毒均对AI审稿评分产生显著的因果影响，揭示了AI审稿的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系与实验审计为评估AI审稿可靠性提供了循证基线，并指出了具体故障点以指导可测试的缓解措施。

Abstract: The volume of scientific submissions continues to climb, outpacing the capacity of qualified human referees and stretching editorial timelines. At the same time, modern large language models (LLMs) offer impressive capabilities in summarization, fact checking, and literature triage, making the integration of AI into peer review increasingly attractive -- and, in practice, unavoidable. Yet early deployments and informal adoption have exposed acute failure modes. Recent incidents have revealed that hidden prompt injections embedded in manuscripts can steer LLM-generated reviews toward unjustifiably positive judgments. Complementary studies have also demonstrated brittleness to adversarial phrasing, authority and length biases, and hallucinated claims. These episodes raise a central question for scholarly communication: when AI reviews science, can we trust the AI referee? This paper provides a security- and reliability-centered analysis of AI peer review. We map attacks across the review lifecycle -- training and data retrieval, desk review, deep review, rebuttal, and system-level. We instantiate this taxonomy with four treatment-control probes on a stratified set of ICLR 2025 submissions, using two advanced LLM-based referees to isolate the causal effects of prestige framing, assertion strength, rebuttal sycophancy, and contextual poisoning on review scores. Together, this taxonomy and experimental audit provide an evidence-based baseline for assessing and tracking the reliability of AI peer review and highlight concrete failure points to guide targeted, testable mitigations.

</details>


### [281] [Thinking Like a Clinician: A Cognitive AI Agent for Clinical Diagnosis via Panoramic Profiling and Adversarial Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23605)
*Zhiqi Lv,Duofan Tu,Jun Li,Mingyue Zhao,Heqin Zhu,Wenliang Li,Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DxChain框架，通过模仿临床医生认知轨迹的链式临床推理（记忆锚定-导航-验证三阶段），结合Profile-Then-Plan范式、医学思维树（Med-ToT）算法和"天使-恶魔"对抗辩论机制，有效缓解LLM在临床决策中的"隧道视野"和诊断幻觉问题，在MIMIC-IV数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床决策支持中处理非结构化电子健康记录时存在严重的"隧道视野"（关注局部信息而忽略全局）和诊断幻觉问题，亟需一种能够模拟临床医生认知过程的结构化推理框架来提升诊断的准确性和逻辑一致性。

Method: 提出DxChain三阶段链式推理框架：(1) Profile-Then-Plan范式，先建立全景患者基线再规划诊断路径，缓解冷启动幻觉；(2) 医学思维树(Med-ToT)算法，实现战略性前瞻规划和资源感知导航；(3) 辩证诊断验证过程，采用"天使-恶魔"对抗辩论机制解决复杂证据冲突。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-Ext心脏病和MIMIC-IV-Ext CDM两个真实世界基准上，DxChain在诊断准确性和逻辑一致性方面均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: DxChain提供了一种模块化、可靠的临床AI架构，通过模拟临床医生的认知轨迹有效缓解LLM的幻觉和隧道视野问题，为下一代临床决策支持系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The application of large language models (LLMs) in clinical decision support faces significant challenges of "tunnel vision" and diagnostic hallucinations present in their processing unstructured electronic health records (EHRs). To address these challenges, we propose a novel chain-based clinical reasoning framework, called DxChain, which transforms the diagnostic workflow into an iterative process by mirroring a clinician's cognitive trajectory that consists of "Memory Anchoring", "Navigation" and "Verification" phases. DxChain introduces three key methodological innovations to elicit the potential of LLM: (i) a Profile-Then-Plan paradigm to mitigate cold-start hallucinations by establishing a panoramic patient baseline, (ii) a Medical Tree-of-Thoughts (Med-ToT) algorithm for strategic look ahead planning and resource aware navigation, and (iii) a Dialectical Diagnostic Verification procedure utilizing "Angel-Devil" adversarial debates to resolve complex evidence conflicts. Evaluated on two real world benchmarks, MIMIC-IV-Ext Cardiac Disease and MIMIC-IV-Ext CDM, DxChain achieves state-of-the-art performances in both diagnostic accuracy and logical consistency, offering a modular and reliable architecture for next-generation clinical AI. The code is at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Dx-Chain.

</details>


### [282] [Tandem: Riding Together with Large and Small Language Models for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23623)
*Zichuan Fu,Xian Wu,Guojing Li,Yejing Wang,Yijun Chen,Zihao Zhao,Yixuan Luo,Hanyu Yan,Yefeng Zheng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Tandem提出了一种大模型与小模型协作的推理框架，大模型仅生成关键的推理线索，小模型基于线索完成完整推理，通过成本感知的早停机制节省约40%计算成本，并在数学推理和代码生成任务上达到与单独大模型相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时生成冗长的中间推理步骤，虽然提升了答案质量和可解释性，但带来了巨大的计算开销。作者希望在不牺牲推理质量的前提下降低计算成本。

Method: Tandem框架由大模型（LLM）作为策略协调者，生成紧凑的关键推理线索；小模型（SLM）基于这些线索执行完整推理并输出最终答案。引入成本感知的早停机制，自适应决定何时停止大模型的生成，平衡效率与可靠性。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，相比单独使用大模型推理，Tandem减少了约40%的计算成本，同时性能达到竞争性或更优水平。充分性分类器可跨领域迁移，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: Tandem通过大模型提供精简推理线索、小模型完成推理的协作方式，有效降低推理成本；成本感知的早停机制能自适应平衡效率与质量，且方法具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have catalyzed the rise of reasoning-intensive inference paradigms, where models perform explicit step-by-step reasoning before generating final answers. While such approaches improve answer quality and interpretability, they incur substantial computational overhead due to the prolonged generation sequences. In this paper, we propose Tandem, a novel collaborative framework that synergizes large and small language models (LLMs and SLMs) to achieve high-quality reasoning with significantly reduced computational cost. Specifically, the LLM serves as a strategic coordinator, efficiently generating a compact set of critical reasoning insights. These insights are then used to guide a smaller, more efficient SLM in executing the full reasoning process and delivering the final response. To balance efficiency and reliability, Tandem introduces a cost-aware termination mechanism that adaptively determines when sufficient reasoning guidance has been accumulated, enabling early stopping of the LLM's generation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that Tandem reduces computational costs by approximately 40% compared to standalone LLM reasoning, while achieving superior or competitive performance. Furthermore, the sufficiency classifier trained on one domain transfers effectively to others without retraining. The code is available at: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/ACL2026_Tandem.

</details>


### [283] [Information-Theoretic Measures in AI: A Practical Decision Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23716)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos,Konstantinos E. Psannis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对七种信息论度量（熵、交叉熵、互信息、转移熵、Φ、EI、自主性），构建了一个包含三个规范性问题的决策框架，并提供了流程图和主决策表，帮助研究者根据数据类型、维度和应用场景选择合适的度量与估计器，避免常见的误用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论度量在AI中广泛应用，但度量选择常与估计器假设、失效模式和安全推断脱节，缺乏系统化的决策指导。

Method: 围绕每个度量的三个规范性问题（回答什么问题/适用AI场景、适合何种数据类型与维度的估计器、最危险的误用），构建决策框架，并通过流程图和决策表进行形式化呈现，辅以三个代表性的应用案例。

Result: 提出了一个可操作的度量选择框架，包含七种度量的标准化Bridge Boxes（连接IT量与认知构念），覆盖AI/ML和决策智能体两个应用域，以及三个具体案例的框架应用演示。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI practitioners提供了从度量选择到估计器匹配再到风险规避的完整指导，有望推动信息论度量的更规范、更安全的应用。

Abstract: Information-theoretic (IT) measures are ubiquitous in artificial intelligence: entropy drives decision-tree splits and uncertainty quantification, cross-entropy is the default classification loss, mutual information underpins representation learning and feature selection, and transfer entropy reveals directed influence in dynamical systems.
  A second, less consolidated family of measures, integrated information (Phi), effective information (EI), and autonomy, has emerged for characterizing agent complexity. Despite wide adoption, measure selection is often decoupled from estimator assumptions, failure modes, and safe inferential claims.
  This paper provides a practical decision framework for all seven measures, organized around three prescriptive questions for each: (i) what question does the measure answer and in which AI context; (ii) which estimator is appropriate for the data type and dimensionality; and (iii) what is the most dangerous misuse. The framework is operationalized in two complementary artifacts: a measure-selection flowchart and a master decision table. We cover both AI/ML and decision-making agent application domains per measure, with standardized Bridge Boxes linking IT quantities to cognitive constructs. Three worked examples illustrate the framework on concrete practitioner scenarios spanning representation learning, temporal influence analysis, and evolved agent complexity.

</details>


### [284] [Expert Evaluation of LLM's Open-Ended Legal Reasoning on the Japanese Bar Exam Writing Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23730)
*Jungmin Choi,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Hiroaki Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了首个用于评估大语言模型在日本法域下开放式法律推理能力的数据集，基于日本司法考试写作部分，通过法律专家人工评估揭示了模型在识别法律争点、构建结构化法律论证以及产生幻觉方面的局限与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多聚焦于LLM在多选题等标准化法律基准上的表现，而对开放式法律推理能力的评估不足；特别是在日本法域下，此前缺乏相关的研究和数据集。

Method: 基于日本司法考试写作部分构建数据集，要求模型从长篇叙述中识别多个法律争点并以自由文本格式构建结构化法律论证；采用法律专家对模型生成回答进行人工评估，并对幻觉现象进行人工分析。

Result: 法律专家评估揭示了当前LLM在法律推理中的局限与挑战；幻觉分析表征了模型何时以及如何引入缺乏先例或法律依据的内容。

Conclusion: 真实考题、模型生成回答及专家评估共同展示了当前LLM在日本法律领域所达到的里程碑；数据集及相关资源将开源提供。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on legal benchmarks, including multiple-choice components of bar exams. However, their capacity for generating open-ended legal reasoning in realistic scenarios remains insufficiently explored. Notably, to our best knowledge, there are no prior studies or datasets addressing this issue in the Japanese context.
  This study presents the first dataset designed to evaluate the open-ended legal reasoning performance of LLMs within the Japanese jurisdiction. The dataset is based on the writing component of the Japanese bar examination, which requires examinees to identify multiple legal issues from long narratives and to construct structured legal arguments in free text format. Our key contribution is the manual evaluation of LLMs' generated responses by legal experts, which reveals limitations and challenges in legal reasoning. Moreover, we conducted a manual analysis of hallucinations to characterize when and how the models introduce content not supported by precedent or law.
  Our real exam questions, model-generated responses, and expert evaluations reveal the milestones of current LLMs in the Japanese legal domain. Our dataset and relevant resources will be available online.

</details>


### [285] [Modeling Induced Pleasure through Cognitive Appraisal Prediction via Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23753)
*Nastaran Dab,Raziyeh Zall,Mohammadreza Kangavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合认知评估理论和模糊模型的计算框架，利用Transformer架构和注意力机制对视频引发的愉悦情绪进行多模态情感计算，解决了标签噪声、语义鸿沟、数据集稀缺和可解释性不足四个挑战，实验最高准确率达0.6624。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感计算缺乏对视觉内容如何影响认知解释并引发特定情感体验（如愉悦）的理解，存在人工标签噪声、"积极情绪"与"愉悦"间的语义鸿沟、愉悦特定数据集稀缺以及黑盒融合方法可解释性差等问题。

Method: 融合数据驱动与认知理论驱动方法，基于认知评估理论和模糊模型构建创新框架；采用Transformer架构和注意力机制进行细粒度多模态特征提取与可解释融合，捕捉模态内和模态间与愉悦相关的动态信息，预测底层评估变量。

Result: 实验验证了该方法在检测视频诱发愉悦方面的有效性，在愉悦水平预测任务上达到0.6624的峰值准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究对情感内容推荐、智能媒体创作以及理解数字媒体对人类情绪的影响具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Multimodal affective computing analyzes user-generated social media content to predict emotional states. However, a critical gap remains in understanding how visual content shapes cognitive interpretations and elicits specific affective experiences such as pleasure. This study introduces a novel computational model to infer video-induced pleasure via cognitive appraisal variables. The proposed model addresses four challenges: (1) noisy and inconsistent human labels, (2) the semantic gap between "positive emotions" and "pleasure," (3) the scarcity of pleasure-specific datasets, and (4) the limited interpretability of existing black-box fusion methods. Our approach integrates data-driven and cognitive theory-driven methods, using cognitive appraisal theory and a fuzzy model within an innovative framework. The model employs transformer-based architectures and attention mechanisms for fine-grained multimodal feature extraction and interpretable fusion to capture both inter- and intra-modal dynamics associated with pleasure. This enables the prediction of underlying appraisal variables, thereby bridging the semantic gap and enhancing model explainability beyond conventional statistical associations. Experimental results validate the efficacy of the proposed method in detecting video-induced pleasure, achieving a peak accuracy of 0.6624 in predicting pleasure levels. These findings highlight promising implications for affective content recommendation, intelligent media creation, and advancing our understanding of how digital media influences human emotions.

</details>


### [286] [FAIR_XAI: Improving Multimodal Foundation Model Fairness via Explainability for Wellbeing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23786)
*Sophie Chiang,Tom Brennan,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Jiaee Cheong,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）在心理健康评估中的公平性与可解释性问题，发现不同模型在实验室和自然场景数据集上表现差异显著，且存在性别和种族偏见；可解释性干预虽能提升过程一致性，但无法保证结果公平，有时甚至会放大偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在心理健康评估中的部署缺乏透明性且存在偏见风险，而现有关于公平性与可解释性AI（XAI）交叉领域的研究尚未充分应用于VLM的健康评估和抑郁预测场景。

Method: 在实验室（AFAR-BSFT）和自然场景（E-DAIC）数据集上评估Phi3.5-Vision和Qwen2-VL两种VLM的诊断准确性与人口统计公平性，并引入XAI干预框架（包括公平性提示和基于可解释性的干预）来评估其对公平性和准确性的影响。

Result: Phi3.5-Vision在E-DAIC上准确率达80.4%，而Qwen2-VL仅33.9%；两种模型在AFAR-BSFT上均倾向于过度预测抑郁；Qwen2-VL存在更显著的性别偏差，Phi-3.5-Vision则表现出更多种族偏差；XAI干预效果不一，公平性提示虽实现了Qwen2-VL的完全机会均等但严重牺牲了准确性，可解释性干预提升了过程一致性却未保证结果公平，有时甚至加剧种族偏见。

Conclusion: 过程透明度与结果公平性之间存在持续差距，未来的公平性干预必须同时优化预测准确性、人口统计公平性和跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: In recent years, the integration of multimodal machine learning in wellbeing assessment has offered transformative potential for monitoring mental health. However, with the rapid advancement of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), their deployment in clinical settings has raised concerns due to their lack of transparency and potential for bias. While previous research has explored the intersection of fairness and Explainable AI (XAI), its application to VLMs for wellbeing assessment and depression prediction remains under-explored. This work investigates VLM performance across laboratory (AFAR-BSFT) and naturalistic (E-DAIC) datasets, focusing on diagnostic reliability and demographic fairness. Performance varied substantially across environments and architectures; Phi3.5-Vision achieved 80.4% accuracy on E-DAIC, while Qwen2-VL struggled at 33.9%. Additionally, both models demonstrated a tendency to over-predict depression on AFAR-BSFT. Although bias existed across both architectures, Qwen2-VL showed higher gender disparities, while Phi-3.5-Vision exhibited more racial bias. Our XAI intervention framework yielded mixed results; fairness prompting achieved perfect equal opportunity for Qwen2-VL at a severe accuracy cost on E-DAIC. On AFAR-BSFT, explainability-based interventions improved procedural consistency but did not guarantee outcome fairness, sometimes amplifying racial bias. These results highlight a persistent gap between procedural transparency and equitable outcomes. We analyse these findings and consolidate concrete recommendations for addressing them, emphasising that future fairness interventions must jointly optimise predictive accuracy, demographic parity, and cross-domain generalisation.

</details>


### [287] [Domain-Filtered Knowledge Graphs from Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23829)
*John Winnicki,Abeynaya Gnanasekaran,Eric Darve*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取的扁平特征清单转化为结构化的内部知识图谱。通过对比激活和多阶段过滤构建领域特定概念宇宙，再构建共现图（捕捉语料级概念结构）和基于transcoder的机制图（链接源层与目标层特征），最后通过自动边标注生成可读知识图谱。在生物学教科书案例中，该方法恢复了章节级结构，揭示了跨主题概念，并将包含数千特征的句子级活动压缩为可读视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE虽然能从语言模型中提取数百万可解释特征，但扁平的特征清单本身实用性有限：领域概念与通用/弱特征混杂，相关概念分散在不同单元中，且无法理解特征间的关系。

Method: 1) 使用对比激活和多阶段过滤从大规模SAE清单中构建严格的领域特定概念宇宙；2) 在过滤集上构建两个对齐的图视图：共现图（多层次粒度组织语料级概念结构）和基于transcoder的机制图（通过稀疏潜在路径连接源层与目标层特征）；3) 自动边标注将图视图转化为可读知识图谱。

Result: 在生物学教科书案例中，这些图谱恢复了连贯的章节和子章节级结构，揭示了连接相邻主题的概念，并将包含数千特征的混乱句子级活动转化为紧凑、可读的视图，展示了模型的局部活动。

Conclusion: 该方法将扁平的SAE清单重塑为内部知识图谱，将特征级可解释性转化为模型知识的全局地图，并支持对推理忠实性的审计。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) extract millions of interpretable features from a language model, but flat feature inventories aren't very useful on their own.
